The viewers group is enough. The timeouts aren't an access problem, and fin-warehouse-readers wouldn't fix them. The charts run on the dashboard's own queries, and your permissions don't change how fast those run. The warehouse group would only add access to raw ledger and payroll detail, and you've told me you don't need that. So I'm not going to swap you over. The warehouse request also still needs Adaeze's approval. The knowledge base says timeouts like this usually come from heavy filters. Here's what to try: - **Shorten the date range.** For example, filter to a single week or a few days instead of the whole month, then compare the pieces. - **Remove broad filters.** Clear any extra filters you don't need on the two slow charts. - **Ask the Finance data team for a cached version.** Adaeze's team owns the data, and a cached copy of the October report would load much faster. You're already messaging Adaeze, so you could ask her at the same time. If the charts still time out after that, tell me which two charts they are and I'll open a ticket with the details. Slow queries are a performance issue, so I can't fix them from the access side.

Payroll is part of Finance in our records. Your HR record lists your old role as Payroll Specialist in the Finance department, so "Finance" is just the department name. The access I removed is the one you're expecting to lose. You don't need to do anything else before you head back to your desk. That stops it from being quarantined again. - **Contact us if something breaks:** If you hit a sign-in problem, message IT Help and we'll sort it out. Enjoy being back!

agent
Ask Teodor. I don't have a list of tools for each role. He's the one who decides what your job needs, and access requests for you have to come from him anyway. - **What you have now:** Your only group right now is `all-staff`, since the Payroll access is gone. So you'll probably need to ask for some Procurement-specific access. - **A quick way to get a list:** Ask Teodor which groups and licenses his team normally has, or look at what a teammate in a similar role uses. He can then send IT Help one request covering everything. - **How the approvals work:** Standard access needs only his approval. Sensitive groups also need the group owner's approval, and privileged ones need Security's. I handle those approvals once he asks. - **When you can message me:** If you're unsure what a particular group or product gives you, tell me its name and I'll tell you what it is and how sensitive it is. That can help you and Teodor decide what to request.
